    CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
    主席報告

    Dear Shareholders,                                                                              致各位股東：

    O n b e h a l f o f t h e b o a r d o f d i r e c t o r s (t h e “B o a r d”) o f D a p h n e   本人謹代表達芙妮國際控股有限公司（「本公司」）連
    International Holdings Limited (the “Company”) together with its                                同其附屬公司（統稱「本集團」）董事會（「董事會」），
    subsidiaries (collectively referred to as the “Group”), I am pleased                            向各位提呈本公司截至二零二三年十二月三十一日
    to present the annual report of the Company for the year ended 31                               止年度之年報。
    December 2023.

    In 2023, the world entered the post-pandemic era, marking a significant                         於二零二三年，全球進入後疫情時代，成為全球復
    milestone in global recovery. However, global economic growth                                   甦的重要里程碑。然而，由於憂慮持續通脹壓力和
    remained soft due to concerns about constant inflationary pressures and                         利率上升，全球經濟增長依然疲弱。雖然中國疫情
    rising interest rate. Although epidemic prevention and control measures                         防控措施已逐步解除，社會日漸復常，消費者信心
    were gradually lifted in China, allowing society to return to normalcy                          在年初有所回升，但宏觀經濟的不確定性阻礙了持
    and sparking a revival in consumer sentiment earlier in the year,                               續強勁的上升勢頭。剔除受疫情影響的三年，中國
    macroeconomic uncertainty hindered strong and consistent momentum.                              於二零二三年經歷了三十年來最緩慢的經濟增速。
    In 2023, China experienced its slowest economic growth in three                                 由於經濟前景不明朗，功能性、實用性、可負擔性
    decades, excluding the three years affected by the epidemic. Due to the                         成為影響消費者購買決定的重要因素。儘管經營環
    cloudy economic outlook, functionality, practicality, and affordability                         境充滿挑戰，本集團持續推動強化「達芙妮」品牌管
    have become important factors influencing consumer purchasing                                   理、優化品牌授權及分銷業務、完善供應鏈系統等
    decisions. Against the backdrop of a challenging operating environment,                         營運策略重點。經過多年努力夯實業務基礎，即使
    the Group continued to advance its strategic focus on strengthening the                         市場復甦慢於預期，本集團業務仍取得顯著增長，
    brand management of “DAPHNE”, optimising the brand licensing and                                表現優於整體市場，彰顯了本集團的有效策略。
    distribution business, and refining the supply chain system. Through
    years of diligent efforts to strengthen its business foundation, despite a
    slower-than-expected market recovery, the Group achieved significant
    growth, outperforming the general market and demonstrating the
    efficacy of its strategic initiatives.

    The momentum of the online shopping trend remained strong                                       全年線上購物趨勢依然強勁，引發了電商格局的改
    throughout the year, sparking a transformative shift in the e-commerce                          變。創新的業界參與者，特別是興趣類電商平台，
    landscape. Innovative players, particularly interest-based e-commerce                           已經席捲電商世界，因此本集團深明企業要適應日
    platforms, have taken the e-commerce world by storm. The Group                                  益激烈的電商競爭之重要性。憑藉有效發揮領先品
    recognises the importance of adapting to the increasingly fierce                                牌的資源優勢，本集團持續優化線上授權網絡，加
    e-commerce competition. By effectively capitalising on its resource                             強數位行銷，並積極拓展與各電商平台的合作。於
    advantages as a leading brand, the Group continued its efforts in                               二零二三年，「達芙妮」品牌在「天貓」及「抖音」的女
    optimising its online licensing network, enhancing digital marketing,                           鞋品類排名均佔領先地位，成功進一步鞏固了本集
    and actively pursuing collaborations with various platforms. These                              團在電商平台的地位。受惠於本集團在多個線上平
    endeavours further strengthened the Group’s position on e-commerce                              台的強大影響力，線上授權業務持續快速增長，為
    platforms, with “DAPHNE” holding a leading position in the women’s                              本集團注入強勁增長動力，並在同業中脫穎而出。
    shoe category on both “Tmall” and “Douyin” in 2023. Benefiting from
    its strong presence on various online platforms, the Group’s online
    licensing business sustained rapid growth, fuelling significant growth
    momentum for the Group and enabling the Group to outshine its peers.

    In response to the ever-changing retail landscape, the Group carefully                          為應對零售格局持續變革，本集團於年內審慎調整
    adjusted the pace of expansion plans for both online licensing network                          線上授權網絡及由加盟商經營的實體店網絡的擴張

     Foreign Exchange Risk Management                                               外匯風險管理
     Foreign exchange risk arises from commercial transactions and                  外匯風險來自於商業交易和以本集團實體經營的功
     recognised assets and liabilities denominated in currencies other than         能貨幣以外的貨幣計值的已確認資產及負債。本集
     the functional currency of the Group’s entities to which they operate.         團主要面對不同貨幣的外匯風險，主要與港元有關。
     The Group is mainly exposed to foreign exchange risk from various
     currencies, primarily with respect to Hong Kong dollars.

     The Group manages its foreign exchange risk by performing regular              本集團透過定期審閱本集團之淨外匯風險，管理本
     reviews of the Group’s net foreign exchange exposures and the Group            集團之外匯風險，且本集團於預期相關外幣出現大

          3.5 Board Practices and Conduct of Meetings                           3.5 董事會常規及會議進程
               The Board holds regular meetings no less than 4 times each         董事會每年定期舉行不少於四次會議，以
               year to formulate overall strategy of the Group, monitor its       制定本集團之整體策略、監察其財務表現
               financial performance and maintain effective oversight over        及對管理層作出有效監督。董事可親身出
               management. Directors may participate either in person or          席或透過電子媒介參與會議。定期董事會
               through electronic means of communications. Notice of              會議之通知會於會議舉行之前至少十四日
               regular board meetings is served to all directors at least 14      送達所有董事。其他董事會及委員會會議
               days prior to the meeting. For other board and committee           一般會發出合理通知。
               meetings, reasonable notice is generally given.

               Agenda of each board meeting is usually sent to all directors      各董事會會議之議程一般會連同會議通知
               together with the notice of meeting in order to give them an       發送予所有董事，讓彼等有機會將任何其
               opportunity to include any other matters in the agenda for         他事項納入議程中以於會議上討論。董事
               discussion in the meeting. Board papers together with all          會文件以及所有合適、完整及可靠之資料
               appropriate, complete and reliable information are sent to         均於每次舉行董事會會議前最少三日發送
               directors at least 3 days before each board meeting to keep        予董事，使彼等獲悉本集團之最新發展及
               the directors apprised of the latest development and financial     財務狀況，以便彼等作出知情之決定。董
               position of the Group and to enable them to make informed          事會及每名董事於有需要時，亦有自行及
               decisions. The Board and each director also have separate          獨立地接觸高級管理人員之途徑。
               and independent access to the senior management whenever
               necessary.

               The Chairman and other relevant senior management                  主席及其他相關之高級管理人員一般會出
               normally attend regular board and committee meetings, and          席常規董事會及委員會會議，並於有需要
               where necessary, other board and committee meetings to             時出席其他董事會及委員會會議，就本集
               advise on business development, financial and accounting           團之業務發展、財務及會計事宜、法定合
               matters, statutory compliance, corporate governance,               規、企業管治、環境、社會及管治議題及
               environmental, social and governance issues and other major        其他主要方面提供意見。每次會議後，一
               aspects of the Group. Draft minutes are normally circulated        般會於合理時間內向所有董事發送會議記
               to all directors for comment in due course after each meeting      錄初稿以供審閱，而最後定稿由公司秘書
               and the final copy, which is kept by the company secretary, is     保存，可供董事查閱。
               open for directors’ inspection.

               Any material transaction, which involves a conflict of             任何涉及主要股東或董事利益衝突之重大
               interests for a substantial shareholder or a director, will be     交易，將在正式召開之董事會會議上由董
               considered and dealt with by the Board at a duly convened          事會審議及處理。除本公司組織章程細則
               board meeting. Except for those circumstances permitted by         允許之情況外，於任何合約、交易、安排
               the Company’s articles of association, a director who has a        或向董事會提呈以供考慮之任何其他類別
               material interest in any contract, transaction, arrangement        之建議中擁有重大權益之董事，將須就相
               or any other kind of proposal put forward to the Board for         關決議案放棄投票，且有關董事不會被計
               consideration shall abstain from voting on the relevant            入為決定法定人數之數目內。
               resolution and such director shall not be counted for quorum
               determination purposes.

               Apart from regular board meetings, the Chairman also held          除常規董事會會議外，主席亦於年內與獨
               a meeting with the independent non-executive directors             立非執行董事舉行一次會議，當時無執行

               year.

               Nomination Procedure and Process                                                    提名程序及過程
               The Nomination Committee will recommend to the Board                                提名委員會將根據以下程序及過程向董事
               for the appointment of a director in accordance with the                            會建議委任董事：
               following procedure and process:

               (a)   by giving due consideration to the current composition                        (a)   在適當考慮董事會的現有成員組成及
                     and size of the Board, develop a list of desirable skills,                          規模後，制定一份合適技能、觀點及
                     perspectives and experience at the outset to focus the                              經驗的清單，以便集中精力物色人選；
                     search effort;

               (b) consult any source it deems appropriate in identifying                          (b) 物色或甄選合適人選時，可向其認為
                   or selecting suitable candidates, such as referrals from                            合適的任何來源作出諮詢，例如現任
                   existing directors, advertising, recommendations                                    董事推介、刊登廣告、獨立第三方代
                   from independent agency firms, and proposals from                                   理人公司推薦及本公司股東的建議，
                   shareholders of the Company, with due consideration                                 並適當參照上文「甄選標準」一節所載
                   given to the criteria set out in the section titled                                 的標準；
                   “Selection Criteria” above;

               (c)   adopt any process it deems appropriate in evaluating                          (c)   評估合適人選時，可採納其認為適當
                     the suitability of the candidates, such as interviews,                              的任何程序，例如面試、背景核查、

    Summary of Board Diversity Policy                                         董事會成員多元化政策概要
    The Company has adopted a board diversity policy (the                     本公司已採納董事會成員多元化政策（「董

    achieve and maintain diversity on the Board in order to                   維持董事會成員多元化以提升董事會效率
    enhance the effectiveness of the Board.                                   之方法。

    Pursuant to the Board Diversity Policy, the Company seeks                 根據董事會成員多元化政策，本公司在考
    to achieve board diversity through having due regard to a                 慮委任新董事時，透過考慮甄選標準中的
    number of diversity factors, including but not limited to                 多項多元化因素，包括但不限於性別、年
    gender, age, cultural and education background, professional              齡、文 化 及 教 育 背 景、專 業 經 驗、技 術、
    experience, skills, knowledge and length of service, in the               知識及服務年期，尋求達致董事會成員多
    selection criteria in considering the appointment of new                  元化。本公司亦將考慮有關自身商業模式
    directors. The Company will also take into account factors                及不時的特殊需求等因素。最終將根據獲
    relating to its own business model and specific needs from                選候選人的優勢及可為董事會帶來的貢獻
    time to time. The ultimate decision is based on merit and                 而作決定。
    contribution that the selected candidates will bring to the
    Board.

  5.3 Risk Management and Internal Control                                 5.3 風險管理及內部監控
      The Board is responsible for risk management (including                  董事會負責本集團之風險管理（包括環境、
      ESG risk) and internal control systems of the Group and for              社會及管治風險）及內部監控系統及檢討其
      reviewing its effectiveness. Such systems are designed to                效益。該系統的制定為管理，而非消除未
      manage rather than eliminate the risk of failure to achieve              能實現經營目標之風險；對重大錯誤陳述
      business objectives, and can only provide reasonable and not             或損失，只能提供合理的，而不是絕對的
      absolute assurance against material misstatement or loss.                保證。

      Aimed at providing reasonable assurance against material                 本公司著力提供合理保證，避免出現重大
      errors, losses or fraud, the Company has established risk                錯誤、損失或欺詐，為此已成立風險管理
      management procedures which comprise the following                       程序，包括以下流程：
      process:

      •    Risk identification: identify major and significant risks           •   風險識別：識別可能影響達成本集團
           that could affect the achievement of goals of the Group;                目標之主要及重大風險；

      •    Risk evaluation and assessment: assess and evaluate                 •   風險評估及評核：根據已識別風險預
           the identified risk according to its likely impact and the              料構成的影響及出現的可能性作出評
           likelihood of occurrence; and                                           估及評核；及

      •    Risk management and mitigation: develop effective                   •   風險管理及緩解：建立有效的監管活
           control activities to mitigate the risks.                               動，務求緩解風險。

      In particular, procedures have been designed for safeguarding            具體而言，已制定程序保障資產不會在未
      assets against unauthorised use or disposition, ensuring the             獲授權下使用或處置；確保適當會計記錄
      maintenance of proper accounting records for the provision               的存置，為內部使用或公開發佈提供可靠
      of reliable financial information for internal use or for                財務資料；及確保遵守適用法律、法規及
      publication, and ensuring compliance of applicable laws,                 規例。
      rules and regulations.

      Risk identification and assessment are performed or updated              風險辨別及評估會每年進行或更新，記錄
      annually, and the results of risk assessment, evaluation and             各職能或營運之風險評核、評估及舒緩的
      mitigation of each function or operation are recorded and                結果，並供董事會及管理層審閱。
      communicated to the Board and management for reviews.

      An internal audit department has been established to conduct             本集團設有內部審核部門，以進行本集團
      risk management and internal audit of the Group. It performs             風險管理及內部審核。內部審核部進行基
      risk-based audits to review the effectiveness of the Group’s             於風險的審核，審閱本集團之風險管理及
      risk management and internal control systems so as to                    內部監控系統之有效性，以保證主要業務
      provide assurance that key business and operational risks                和營運之風險已被識別和管理。內部審核
      are identified and managed. The work carried out by the                  部每年最少向審核委員會報告兩次其開展
      internal audit department reports to the Audit Committee                 之工作，並提供調查結果及建議。截至二
      with its findings and makes recommendations at least twice               零二三年十二月三十一日止年度，董事會
      a year. During the year ended 31 December 2023, the Board                已就本集團之風險管理及內部監控系統之
      has conducted a review of the effectiveness of the risk                  有效性進行檢討，包括財務、營運及合規
      management and internal control systems of the Group,                    監控及風險管理職能。
      including financial, operational and compliance controls and
      risk management functions.

      The Company considers its risk management and internal                   本公司認為其風險管理及內部監控系統有
      control systems effective and adequate.                                  效及恰當。

               Anti-corruption Policy                                               反貪污政策
               The Board has adopted an anti-corruption policy (the “Anti-          董 事 會 已 採 納 反 貪 污 政 策（「反 貪 污 政
               corruption Policy”). The Group is committed to achieving             策」）。本集團致力在經營其業務方面達致
               the highest standards of integrity and ethical behaviour in          最高誠信及道德行為標準。反貪污政策構
               conducting its business. The Anti-corruption Policy forms an         成本集團企業管治框架的重要部分。反貪
               integral part of the Group’s corporate governance framework.         污政策載列本集團員工及業務夥伴必須遵
               The Anti-corruption Policy sets out the specific behavioural         守以打擊貪污的具體行為指引。這表明本
               guidelines that the Group’s personnel and business partners          集團對踐行合乎道德的商業行為以及遵守
               must follow to combat corruption. It demonstrates the                適用於其本地及海外業務的反貪污法律
               Group’s commitment to the practice of ethical business               及法規的承諾。為貫徹此承諾及確保本集

       Disclosure of Inside Information                                                          披露內幕消息
       The Group has in place a policy on disclosure of inside                                   本集團備有內幕消息披露政策，載列處理
       information which sets out the procedures and internal                                    及發放內幕消息的程序及內部監控措施。
       controls for handling and dissemination of inside
       information.

       The policy provides guidelines to the directors, officers                                 政策為向董事、高級職員及本集團所有有
       and all relevant employees of the Group to ensure proper                                  關 僱 員 提 供 指 引，確 保 有 恰 當 的 保 護 措
       safeguards exist to prevent the Company from breaching the                                施，以免本公司違反法定披露要求。政策
       statutory disclosure requirements. It also includes appropriate                           亦包括適當的內部監控及申報系統，以辨
       internal control and reporting systems to identify and assess                             別及評估潛在的內幕消息。
       potential inside information.

       Key procedures in place include:                                                          已設有的主要步驟包括：

       •    defining the requirements of periodic financial and                                  •   向董事會及公司秘書界定定期財務及
            operational reporting to the Board and company                                           經營申報的規定，致使彼等可評估內
            secretary to enable them to assess inside information                                    幕消息及作適時披露（如有需要）；
            and make timely disclosures, if necessary;

       •    controlling the access to inside information by                                      •   按須知基準控制僱員獲悉內幕消息的
            employees on a need-to-know basis, and safeguarding                                      途徑，及向公眾恰當披露前確保內幕
            the confidentiality of the inside information before it is                               消息絕對保密；及
            properly disclosed to public; and

       •    p r o c e d u r e s o f c o m m u n i c a t i n g w i t h t h e G r o u p’s          •   與本集團持份者（包括股東、投資者、
            stakeholders, including shareholders, investors,                                         分析師等）溝通步驟，方式均遵從上市
            analysts, etc. in ways which are in compliance with the                                  規則。
            Listing Rules.

       The Group has also established and implemented procedures                                 本集團亦已設立及實行步驟，處理外部人
       to handle enquiries from external parties related to the market                           士有關市場傳言及其他涉及本集團事務的
       rumours and other affairs of the Group.                                                   查詢。

       To avoid uneven dissemination of inside information, the                                  為避免出現不公平發放內幕消息，本公司
       dissemination of inside information of the Company shall                                  發放內幕消息時，會於本公司及香港交易
       be conducted by publishing the relevant information on the                                所披露易各自的網站刊載有關資料。
       respective websites of the Company and the HKEXnews.

  7.3 Dividend Policy                                                      7.3 股息政策
      The Company has adopted a dividend policy (the “Dividend                 本 公 司 已 採 納 股 息 政 策（「股 息 政 策」）。
      Policy”). According to the Dividend Policy, in deciding                  根據股息政策，在決定是否建議派發股息
      whether to propose a dividend and in determining the                     及釐定股息金額時，董事會須考慮下列因
      dividend amount, the Board shall take into account, inter alia,          素，其中包括：
      the following factors:

      (i)   the general financial condition of the Group;                      (i)   本集團的一般財務狀況；

      (ii) the Group’s actual and future operations and liquidity              (ii) 本集團的實際和未來營運及流動資金
           position;                                                                狀況；

      (iii) the Group’s expected working capital requirements and              (iii) 本集團預期營運資金需求及未來擴展
            future expansion plans;                                                  計劃；

      (iv) the Group’s debt to equity ratios and the debt level;               (iv) 本集團的負債對權益比率及債務水平；

      (v) the retained profits and distributable reserves of the               (v) 本公司及本集團各成員公司的保留盈
          Company and each of the members of the Group;                            利和可供分派儲備；

      (vi) the shareholders’ and the investors’ expectation and                (vi) 股東及投資者的期望及行業的常規；
           industry’s norm;

      (vii) the general market conditions; and                                 (vii) 一般市場情況；及

      (viii) any other factors that the Board deems appropriate.               (viii) 董事會認為適當的任何其他因素。

      Dividends may be declared and paid to the shareholders of                股息可以現金方式或董事會認為適當的其

a guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with HKSAs will            一重大錯誤陳述存在時總能發現。錯誤陳述可以由
always detect a material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements can      欺詐或錯誤引起，如果合理預期它們單獨或滙總起

     from error, as fraud may involve collusion, forgery, intentional            致的重大錯誤陳述的風險高於未能發現因錯誤

     We communicate with the Audit Committee regarding, among other               除其他事項外，我們與審計委員會溝通了計劃的審

     we identify during our audit.

     complied with relevant ethical requirements regarding independence,          關獨立性的相關專業道德要求，並與他們溝通有可
     and to communicate with them all relationships and other matters that        能合理地被認為會影響我們獨立性的所有關係和其
     may reasonably be thought to bear on our independence, and where             他事項，以及在適用的情況下，用以消除對獨立性
     applicable, actions taken to eliminate threats or safeguards applied.        產生威脅的行動或採取的防範措施。

    in licensing, distribution and sale of footwear products and                產品及配件的授權許可、分銷及銷售業務。
    accessories in Mainland China.

    Kong Limited. The address of its registered office is Cricket               之註冊辦事處位於Cricket Square, Hutchins Drive,

    Cayman Islands.                                                             Islands。

    INFORMATION
    This note provides a list of the accounting policies adopted in the         本附註表列了編製這些合併財務報表時所採用

    2.1 Basis of preparation                                                    2.1 編製基準
         The consolidated financial statements of the Group have been               本集團綜合財務報表乃按照所有適用的香
         prepared in accordance with Hong Kong Financial Reporting                  港財務報告準則（「香港財務報告準則」）、
         Standards (“HKFRSs”), which collectively includes all                      香港公認會計原則及香港公司條例之規定
         applicable individual HKFRSs, Hong Kong Accounting                         而編製。香港財務報告準則包括香港會計
         Standards (“HKASs”) and Interpretations issued by the Hong                 師公會（「香港會計師公會」）頒佈之所有適
         Kong Institute of Certified Public Accountants (“HKICPA”),                 用個別香港財務報告準則、香港會計準則
         accounting principles generally accepted in Hong Kong and                 （「香港會計準則」）及詮釋。除按公平價值
         the requirements of the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.                     計入損益的金融資產按公平價值計量外，
         The consolidated financial statements have been prepared                   綜合財務報表乃按歷史成本法編製。
         under the historical cost convention, except for financial
         assets at fair value through profit or loss which is measured at
         fair value.

         The preparation of consolidated financial statements in                    編製符合香港財務報告準則之綜合財務報
         conformity with HKFRSs requires the use of certain critical                表需要運用若干關鍵會計估計。管理層亦
         accounting estimates. It also requires management to                       須在應用本集團會計政策時作出判斷。涉
         exercise its judgement in the process of applying the Group’s              及較高程度判斷或複雜性的領域，或假設
         accounting policies. The areas involving a higher degree                   及估計對綜合財務報表具有重大意義的領
         of judgement or complexity, or areas where assumptions                     域於附註4披露。
         and estimates are significant to the consolidated financial
         statements, are disclosed in Note 4.

          2.2 Change in presentation currency                                                2.2 更改呈列貨幣
               During the year, the Group has changed its presentation                         於年內，本集團就編製其綜合財務報表已
               currency from Hong Kong dollars (“HK$”) to RMB for the                          將其呈列貨幣由港元（「港元」）更改為人民
               preparation of its consolidated financial statements.                           幣。

               As the principal activities of the Group are mainly conducted                   由於本集團的主要業務活動主要於中國大
               in Mainland China where the functional currencies of those                      陸進行，而該等附屬公司的功能貨幣以人
               subsidiaries are RMB, the directors of the Company consider                     民幣計值，故本公司董事認為，有關更改
               that the change would result in a more relevant presentation                    將促使本集團的交易於綜合財務報表內得
               of the Group’s transactions in the consolidated financial                       到更好的呈列。
               statements.

               Following the change in presentation currency, the financial                    於更改呈列貨幣後，過往報告的財務資料
               information as previously reported has been retranslated                        已根據香港會計準則第8號的條文「會計政
               in accordance with the provisions in HKAS 8 “Accounting                         策、會計估計變動及錯誤」及香港會計準則
               Policies, Changes in Accounting Estimates and Errors” and                       第21號的條文「外匯匯率變動的影響」重新
               HKAS 21 “The Effects of Changes in Foreign Exchange                             換算，猶如人民幣一直為本集團的呈列貨
               Rates” as if RMB had always been the Group’s presentation                       幣，除非無法如此換算。
               currency unless it is impracticable to do so.

               Hence, the Group’s financial information as previously                          因此，本集團過往報告的財務資料已使用
               reported has been re-translated using the procedures outlined                   下列程序重新換算：
               below:

               •    Assets and liabilities of foreign operations where the                     •   功能貨幣並非為人民幣之海外經營資
                    functional currency is not RMB have been translated                            產及負債已按有關期末匯率換算為人
                    into RMB at the relevant closing rates of exchange;                            民幣；

               •    Share capital, share premium and other reserves                            •   按港元計值之股本、股份溢價及其他
                    denominated in HK$ have been translated at historical                          儲備已按交易日期之歷史匯率進行換
                    rates prevailing at the dates of transactions;                                 算；

               •    As for retained profits and currency translation reserve,                  •   關 於 保 留 盈 利 及 匯 兌 儲 備，由 於 無
                    since it is impracticable to reproduce the financial                           法重新編製一九九九年一月一日前
                    records before 1 January 1999, the directors of the                            之 財 務 記 錄，故 本 公 司 董 事 只 能 自
                    Company apply the change in presentation currency                              一九九九年一月一日起應用更改呈
                    prospectively from 1 January 1999. The retained profits                        列貨幣。於一九九九年一月一日功能
                    arising from the Group’s entities whose functional                             貨幣並非人民幣之本集團實體產生
                    c u r r e n c y w a s n o t R M B o n 1 J a n u a r y 1999 w e r e             之保留盈利按於一九九八年十二月
                    translated into RMB at the closing rate of exchange on                         三十一日之期末匯率換算為人民幣。
                    31 December 1998. Profit and loss items since 1 January                        自一九九九年一月一日起之損益項目
                    1999 have been translated into RMB at the relevant                             已按有關平均匯率換算為人民幣。重
                    average rates of exchange. Differences arising from                            新換算資產淨值所產生的差額於滙兌
                    the retranslation of the net assets are recognised in the                      儲備中確認。
                    currency translation reserve.

               In addition to the comparative information in respect of                        除涉及過往期間於綜合財務報表提供之比
               the previous period provided in the consolidated financial                      較資料外，根據香港會計準則第1號「財務

               balance sheet as at 1 January 2022 due to the change                            列於二零二二年一月一日之額外綜合資產

              The consolidated financial statements including the                                       綜合財務報表包括本公司及其所有附

            (i)   Subsidiaries                                                                       (i)   附屬公司
                  Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured                                      附屬公司為所有本集團擁有控制
                  entities) over which the Group has control. The                                          權之實體（包括結構性實體）。當
                  Group controls an entity when the Group is                                               本集團通過干預該實體以得到不
                  exposed to, or has rights to, variable returns from                                      同的回報，及通過對該實體的權
                  its involvement with the entity and has the ability                                      力影響該等回報時，本集團則對
                  to affect those returns through its power over the                                       該實體擁有控制權。
                  entity.

                  Subsidiaries are fully consolidated from the date                                        附屬公司由其控制權轉讓予本集
                  on which control is transferred to the Group. They                                       團之日起全面綜合入賬，並由該
                  are deconsolidated from the date that control                                            控制權終止之日起終止綜合入賬。
                  ceases.

                  Inter-company transactions, balances and                                                 集團內公司之間的交易、結餘及
                  unrealised gains on transactions between group                                           交易的未變現收益予以對銷。未
                  companies are eliminated. Unrealised losses are                                          變現虧損亦予以撇除，除非該交
                  also eliminated unless the transaction provided                                          易提供所轉讓資產減值的證據。
                  evidence of an impairment of the transferred asset.                                      附屬公司的會計政策已按需要變
                  Accounting policies of subsidiaries have been                                            更，以確保與本集團採用的政策
                  changed where necessary to ensure consistency                                            符合一致。
                  with the policies adopted by the Group.

                  The Group applies the acquisition method                                                 本集團採用會計收購法計算業務
                  t o a c c o u n t f o r b u s i n e s s c o m b i n a t i o n s. T h e                   合併。收購附屬公司的轉讓代價
                  consideration transferred for the acquisition of a                                       為所轉讓資產、對被收購方原股
                  subsidiary is the fair values of the assets transferred,                                 東所產生負債及本集團所發行股
                  the liabilities incurred to former shareholders of                                       本權益的公平價值。所轉讓代價
                  the acquiree and the equity interests issued by the                                      包括或然代價安排產生的任何資
                  Group. The consideration transferred includes                                            產或負債的公平價值。於業務合
                  the fair value of any asset or liability resulting                                       併時所收購的可辨別資產及所承
                  from a contingent consideration arrangement.                                             擔的負債及或然負債，初步按收
                  Identifiable assets acquired and liabilities and                                         購日的公平價值計量。本集團按
                  contingent liabilities assumed in a business                                             逐項收購基準確認任何被收購方
                  combination are measured initially at their fair                                         之非控制性權益。當被收購方之
                  values at the acquisition date. The Group recognises                                     非控制性權益為現存擁有權權益
                  any non-controlling interest in the acquiree on an                                       並賦予其持有人於清盤時按相應
                  acquisition-by-acquisition basis. Non-controlling                                        比例分配該實體之資產淨值，本
                  interests in the acquiree that are present ownership                                     集團以公平價值或現存擁有權權
                  interests and entitle their holders to a proportionate                                   益按比例應佔被收購方已確認可
                  share of the entity’s net assets in the event of                                         辨別資產淨值，計量被收購方之
                  liquidation are measured at either fair value or                                         非控制性權益。所有其他非控制
                  the present ownership interests’ proportionate                                           性權益的組件以其收購日公平價

                  identifiable net assets. All other components of non-                                    另有規定。收購相關成本於產生
                  controlling interests are measured at their fair values                                  時列為開支。
                  at acquisition date, unless another measurement
                  basis is required by HKFRSs. Acquisition-related
                  costs are expensed as incurred.

                          ventures depending on the contractual rights                        者的合約權利及義務分類為合資
                          and obligations of each investor. The Group has                     業務或合資企業。本集團已評估
                          assessed the nature of its joint arrangements                       合資安排的性質及將合資安排釐
                          and determined them to be joint ventures. Joint                     定為合資企業。合資企業使用權
                          ventures are accounted for using the equity                         益法入賬。
                          method.

                          Under the equity method of accounting, interests                    根據會計權益法，於合資企業之
                          in joint ventures are initially recognised at cost                  權益初步按成本值確認及其後調
                          and adjusted thereafter to recognise the Group’s                    整，以確認本集團應佔收購後損
                          share of the post-acquisition profits or losses and                 益及其他全面收益變動。當本集
                          movements in other comprehensive income. When                       團應佔合資企業虧損等於或超過
                          the Group’s share of loss in a joint venture equals                 其於合資企業之權益（包括實質
                          or exceeds its interest in the joint venture (which                 上構成本集團於合資企業之投資
                          includes any long-term interests that, in substance,                淨 額 一 部 份 的 任 何 長 期 權 益），
                          form part of the Group’s net investment in the joint                本集團並不再繼續確認虧損，除
                          venture), the Group does not recognise further                      非已產生責任或代表合資企業作
                          loss, unless it has incurred obligations or made                    出付款。
                          payments on behalf of the joint venture.

                          Unrealised gains on transactions between the                        本集團與合資企業之間交易的未
                          Group and its joint ventures are eliminated to the                  變現收益予以撇除，以本集團於
                          extent of the Group’s interests in the joint ventures.              合資企業權益為限。未變現虧損
                          Unrealised losses are also eliminated unless the                    亦予撇除，除非該交易提供所轉
                          transaction provides evidence of an impairment of                   讓資產減值之證據。合資企業的
                          the asset transferred. Accounting policies of the                   會計政策在需要時已予變動，以
                          joint ventures have been changed where necessary                    確保與本集團採納的政策一致。
                          to ensure consistency with the policies adopted by
                          the Group.

                     of business.

                    unless they contain significant financing components,              分，並按公平價值確認。本集團持有
                    when they are recognised at fair value. The Group                  貿易及其他應收賬款，目的是收取合
                    holds the trade and other receivables with the objective           約現金流量，因此其後採用實際利率
                    to collect the contractual cash flows and therefore                法按攤銷成本計量。有關本集團貿易
                    measures them subsequently at amortised cost using the             及其他應收賬款會計處理的進一步資
                    effective interest method. See Note 3.1(iii) for further           料，以及本集團減值政策的描述，請

        2.3.11 Current and deferred income taxes                                   2.3.11 即期及遞延所得稅項
               Income tax for the year comprises current and deferred                     本年度所得稅項包括即期及遞延稅
               tax. Income tax is recognised in the consolidated income                   項。所得稅項於綜合收益表確認，除
               statement, except to the extent that it relates to items                   與於其他全面收益或直接於權益確認
               recognised in other comprehensive income or directly                       之項目相關。在該情況下，所得稅項
               in equity. In this case, the income tax is also recognised                 分別於其他全面收益或直接於權益中
               in other comprehensive income or directly in equity                        確認。
               respectively.

             (i)   Current income tax                                                 (i)   即期所得稅
                   Current income tax charge is calculated on the                           即期所得稅開支按本集團經營所
                   basis of the tax laws enacted or substantively                           在及產生應課稅收入之地方於結
                   enacted at the balance sheet date in the places                          算日已實施或大致上實施之稅法
                   where the Group operates and generates taxable                           計算。管理層定期評估就適用稅
                   income. Management periodically evaluates                                項法規有待詮釋的情況的稅項申
                   positions taken in tax returns with respect to                           報情況，並在適當情況下按預期
                   situations in which applicable tax regulation is                         將支付予稅務機構的款額為基準
                   subject to interpretation and establishes provisions                     確定撥備。
                   where appropriate on the basis of amounts
                   expected to be paid to the tax authorities.

             (ii) Deferred income tax                                                 (ii) 遞延所得稅項
                  Deferred income tax is recognised, using the                             遞延所得稅項乃就資產與負債之
                  liability method, on temporary differences arising                       稅基及其於綜合財務報表中之賬
                  between the tax bases of assets and liabilities                          面值兩者之暫時差額，以負債法
                  and their carrying amounts in the consolidated                           確認。然而，如在初步確認商譽
                  financial statements. However, deferred tax                              時產生的遞延稅項負債，不予確
                  liabilities are not recognised if they arise from                        認；倘遞延所得稅項乃產生自於
                  the initial recognition of goodwill, the deferred                        （業務合併除外）初步確認資產或
                  income tax is not accounted for if it arises from                        負債之交易，而交易當時並無影
                  initial recognition of an asset or liability in a                        響會計或應課稅盈利或虧損，則
                  transaction other than a business combination                            不會列賬。遞延所得稅項乃以於
                  that at the time of the transaction affects neither                      結算日已實施或大致上實施之稅
                  accounting nor taxable profit or loss. Deferred                          率（及稅法）釐定，並預期於變現
                  income tax is determined using tax rates (and laws)                      相關遞延所得稅項資產或結算遞
                  that have been enacted or substantively enacted                          延所得稅項負債時適用。
                  at the balance sheet date and are expected to
                  apply when the related deferred income tax asset
                  is realised or the deferred income tax liability is
                  settled.

                   Deferred income tax assets are recognised                                遞延所得稅項資產按可能出現可
                   only to the extent that it is probable that future                       用作抵銷暫時差額之日後應課稅
                   taxable profit will be available against which the                       盈利之程度來確認。
                   temporary differences can be utilised.

                  of ownership.

        2.3.16 Leases                                                             2.3.16 租賃
               Leases are recognised as right-of-use assets and                          租賃在租賃資產可供本集團使用當日
               corresponding liabilities at the date at which the leased                 確認為使用權資產和相應負債。
               asset is available for use by the Group.

             Contracts may contain both lease and non-lease                          合同可能同時包含租賃和非租賃組成
             components. The Group allocates the consideration                       部分。本集團基於各租賃與非租賃組
             in the contract to the lease and non-lease components                   成部分的單獨價格相對比例分攤合同
             based on their relative stand-alone prices. However,                    對價。不過，對於本集團作為承租人
             for leases of real estate for which the Group is a lessee,              的房地產租賃，本集團已選擇不拆分
             it has elected not to separate lease and non-lease                      租賃與非租賃組成部分，而是將其作
             components and instead accounts for these as a single                   為一項單一的租賃組成部分進行會計
             lease component.                                                        處理。

             Assets and liabilities arising from leases are initially                租賃產生的資產和負債按現值進行初
             measured on a present value basis. Lease liabilities                    始計量。租賃負債包括以下租賃付款
             include the net present value of the following lease                    額的淨現值：
             payments:

             •     fixed payments (including in-substance fixed                      •   固定付款額（包括實質固定付款
                   payments), less any lease incentives receivable;                      額），扣除任何應收的租賃激勵；

             •     variable lease payments that are based on an index                •   基於指數或比率確定的可變租賃
                   or a rate, initially measured using the index or rate                 付款額，採用租賃期開始日的指
                   as at the commencement date;                                          數或比率進行初始計量；

             •     amounts expected to be payable by the Group                       •   本集團根據餘值擔保預計應付的
                   under residual value guarantees;                                      金額；

             •     the exercise price of a purchase option if the Group              •   本集團合理確定將行使的購買選
                   is reasonably certain to exercise that option; and                    擇權的行使價；及

             •     payments of penalties for terminating the lease, if               •   在租賃期反映出本集團將行使選
                   the lease term reflects the Group exercising that                     擇權的情況下終止租賃的罰款金
                   option.                                                               額。

             Lease payments to be made under reasonably certain                      當本集團合理確定將行使續租選擇權
             extension options are also included in the measurement                  時，租賃付款額也納入負債的計量中。
             of the liability.

               2.3.16 Leases (Continued)                                                 2.3.16 租賃（續）
                      The lease payments are discounted using the interest                      租賃付款額按租賃內含利率折現。本
                      rate implicit in the lease. If that rate cannot be readily                集團的租賃內含利率通常無法直接確
                      determined, which is generally the case for leases in the                 定，在此情況下，應採用承租人的增
                      Group, the lessee’s incremental borrowing rate is used,                   量借款利率，即承租人在類似經濟環
                      being the rate that the individual lessee would have                      境下獲得與使用權資產價值接近的資
                      to pay to borrow the fund necessary to obtain an asset                    產，在類似期間以類似抵押條件借入
                      of similar value to the right-of-use asset in a similar                   資金而必須支付的利率。
                      economic environment with similar terms, security and
                      conditions.

                    To determine the incremental borrowing rate, the                       為確定增量借款利率，本集團以個別
                    Group uses recent third-party financing received by                    承租人最近收到的第三方融資為起
                    the individual lessee as a starting point, adjusted to                 點，並進行調整以反映融資條件自收
                    reflect changes in financing conditions since third party              到第三方融資後的變化。
                    financing was received.

                    Lease payments are allocated between principal and                     租賃付款額在本金和融資費用之間進
                    finance cost. The finance cost is charged to profit or loss            行分攤。融資費用在租賃期內計入損
                    over the lease period so as to produce a constant periodic             益，以按照固定的週期性利率對各期
                    rate of interest on the remaining balance of the liability             間負債餘額計算利息。
                    for each period.

                    Right-of-use assets are measured at cost comprising the                使用權資產按成本計量，包括：
                    following:

                    •     the amount of the initial measurement of lease                   •   租賃負債的初始計量金額；
                          liability;

                    •     any lease payments made at or before the                         •   在租賃期開始日或之前支付的租
                          commencement date less any lease incentives                          賃付款額扣除收到的租賃激勵；
                          received;

                    •     any initial direct costs; and                                    •   初始直接費用；及

                    •     restoration costs.                                               •   復原成本。

                    Right-of-use assets are generally depreciated over the                 使用權資產一般在資產的使用壽命與
                    shorter of the asset’s useful life and the lease term on               租賃期兩者孰短的期間內按直線法計
                    a straight-line basis. If the Group is reasonably certain              提折舊。如本集團合理確定會行使購
                    to exercise a purchase option, the right-of-use asset is               買權，則在標的資產的使用壽命期間
                    depreciated over the underlying asset’s useful life.                   內對使用權資產計提折舊。

                    Payments associated with leases of low-value assets                    與低價值資產租賃相關的付款額按直
                    are recognised on a straight-line basis as an expense in               線法確認為費用，計入損益。短期租
                    profit or loss. Short-term leases are leases with a lease              賃是指租賃期為十二個月或者更短的
                    term of 12 months or less. Low-value assets comprise                   租賃。低價值資產包括辦公設備。
                    office equipment.

        2.3.17 Convertible notes                                                    2.3.17 可換股債券
               Convertible notes that can be converted into equity                         可由持有人選擇轉換為權益股本而於
               share capital at the option of the holder, where the                        轉換時發行之股份數目及所收到代價
               number of shares that would be issued on conversion                         之價值並無變動之可換股債券，乃作
               and the value of the consideration that would be                            為包含負債部分及權益部分之複合金
               received do not vary, are accounted for as compound                         融工具列賬。
               financial instruments, which contain both a liability
               component and an equity component.

             At initial recognition the liability component of the                     於初始確認時，可換股債券之負債部
             convertible notes is determined using market interest                     分使用對等之非可換股工具之市場利
             rate for an equivalent non-convertible instrument. The                    率釐定。所得款項餘額乃作為權益部
             remainder of the proceeds is allocated to the conversion                  分分配至換股權。與發行複合金融工
             option as equity component. Transaction costs that                        具相關之交易成本按所得款項之分配
             relate to the issue of the compound financial instrument                  比例分配至負債及權益部分。
             are allocated to the liability and equity components in
             proportion to the allocation of proceeds.

             The liability component is subsequently carried at                        負債部分其後按攤銷成本列賬，並按
             amortised cost, calculated using the effective interest                   實際利率法計算，直至轉換或到期為
             method, until extinguished on conversion or maturity.                     止。權益部分則於可換股債券被轉換
             The equity component is recognised in equity, net of                      或贖回前並扣除任何稅務影響後，於
             any tax effects, until either the convertible notes are                   權益確認。
             converted or redeemed.

             If the convertible notes are converted, the relevant                      倘可換股債券獲轉換，相關權益部分
             equity component and the carrying amount of the                           及負債部分於轉換時之賬面值會就所
             liability component at the time of conversion are                         發行股份轉撥至股本及股份溢價。倘
             transferred to share capital and share premium for the                    可換股債券獲贖回，則權益部分會轉
             shares issued. If the convertible notes are redeemed, the                 撥至保留盈利。
             equity component is transferred to retained profits.

        2.3.18 Dividend distribution                                                2.3.18 股息分派
               Dividend distribution to shareholders of the Company                        向本公司股東分派之股息乃於董事會
               is recognised as a liability in the Company’s and the                       及本公司股東（如適用）批准股息之期
               Group’s financial statements in the period in which the                     間內在本公司及本集團財務報表確認
               dividends are approved by the board of directors and the                    為負債。
               shareholders of the Company, where appropriate.

                         has transferred, being when the products are                            認，即貨品送抵客戶，且不存在

                          Revenue from these sales is recognised based                            收益是根據合同約定的價格扣除

                          estimated discounts. No element of financing is                         排除融資成分的存在。

    2.4 Summary of other accounting policies                                 2.4 其他會計政策概要
        2.4.1 Offsetting financial instruments                                  2.4.1 抵銷金融工具
              Financial assets and liabilities are offset and the net                 倘有抵銷已確認金額的法定可執行權
              amount is reported in the balance sheet when there                      力，且有意按淨額基準結算或變現資
              is a legally enforceable right to offset the recognised                 產並同時結算負債時，則金融資產與
              amount and there is an intention to settle on a net                     負債將相互抵銷，淨額於資產負債表
              basis or realise the asset and settle the liability                     內呈報。這法定可執行權力不能取決
              simultaneously. The legally enforceable right must not                  於將來發生之事件，但必須執行於日
              be contingent on future events and must be enforceable                  常業務及當本集團或交易方出現違
              in the normal course of business and in the event of                    約、無力償還或破產事件。
              default, insolvency or bankruptcy of the Group or the
              counterparty.

        2.4.2 Provisions                                                        2.4.2 撥備
              Provisions are recognised when the Group has a present                  倘本集團因過往事件而須承擔現有法
              obligation (legal or constructive) as a result of past                  定或推定責任，而履行該責任時有可
              events and it is probable that an outflow of economic                   能涉及經濟資源流失，並能可靠地衡
              resources will be required to settle the obligation; and                量涉及金額，則確認有關撥備。不會
              the amount has been reliably estimated. Provisions are                  就未來經營虧損確認撥備。
              not recognised for future operating losses.

             Where there are a number of similar obligations, the                  倘有多項類似責任，於釐定履行有關
             likelihood that an outflow of economic resources will                 責任而引致經濟資源流出之可能性
             be required in settlement is determined by considering                時，會整體考慮該等責任類別。即使
             the class of obligations as a whole. A provision is                   同一責任類別所包含任何一個項目之
             recognised even if the likelihood of an outflow with                  資源流出之可能性極低，仍須確認撥
             respect to any one item included in the same class of                 備。
             obligations may be small.

             Provisions are measured at the present value of the                   撥備乃按履行該責任預計所需開支以
             expenditures expected to be required to settle the                    除稅前貼現率計算之現值計量，有關
             obligations using a pre-tax that reflects current market              貼現率反映當時市場對貨幣時間值及
             assessments of the time value of money and the risks                  該責任特定風險之評估。因時間流逝
             specific to the obligation. The increase in the provision             而增加之撥備確認為利息支出。
             due to passage of time is recognised as interest expense.

        2.4.3 Government subsidies                                              2.4.3 政府補貼
              Government subsidies are recognised at their fair value              政府補助於有合理保證會收到補貼並
              where there is reasonable assurance that the incentives              且所有附加條件均獲遵守之情況下按
              will be received and all attaching conditions will be                其公平價值確認。
              complied with.

        2.4.4 Earnings per share                                                2.4.4 每股盈利
              (i) Basic earnings per share                                            (i) 每股基本盈利
                  Basic earnings per share is calculated by dividing                      每股基本盈利計算乃按本公司股
                  the profit attributable to shareholders of the                          東應佔盈利，除以本財政年度已
                  Company by the weighted average number of                               發行普通股的加權平均數。
                  ordinary shares outstanding during the financial
                  year.

               The Group’s business activities expose it to a variety of               本集團業務活動面臨多項財務風險：外匯
               financial risks: foreign exchange risk, cash flow interest rate         風險、現金流量利率風險、信貸風險及流
               risk, credit risk and liquidity risk. The Group’s overall risk          動資金風險。本集團之整體風險管理針對
               management focuses on the unpredictability of financial                 金融市場之難以預測性，並尋求減低對本
               markets and seeks to minimise potential adverse effect on               集團財務表現構成之潛在不利影響。使用
               the Group’s financial performance. The use of financial                 金融衍生工具管理若干風險需由董事會批
               derivatives to manage certain risk exposures should be                  准。
               approved by the board of directors.

               (i)   Foreign exchange risk                                             (i)   外匯風險

                     denominated in currencies other than the functional                     已確認資產及負債。本集團面臨來自
                     currency of the Group’s entities to which they operate.                 多 種 貨 幣（主 要 涉 及 港 元）的 外 匯 風
                     The Group is exposed to foreign exchange risk from                      險。

              Level 1: The fair value of financial instruments traded                       第一層：在活躍市場交易的金融工具

              used for the financial assets is the current bid price.                       價。這些工具包括在第一層。

              rely as little as possible on entity-specific estimates.                      依賴針對實體的特定估計。如果該工
              If all significant inputs required to fair value of the                       具的公平價值所需的所有重要資料都
              instruments are observable, the instruments are included                      是可觀察的，那麼該工具被列入第二
              in level 2.                                                                   層。

              on observable market data, the instruments are included in                    非基於可觀察的市場資料，則該工具
              level 3.                                                                      被列入第三層。

  The operation of certain directly-managed stores were loss making        於截至二零二三年十二月三十一日止年度，若
  during the year ended 31 December 2023. Management regards               干直營店的經營出現虧損。管理層將每家直營
  each individual directly-managed store as a separate group of            店視為獨立組別的現金產生單位，並通過釐定
  cash generating units and carried out impairment assessment by           有減值迹象的直營店資產的可收回金額進行減
  determining the recoverable amounts of the directly-managed              值評估。可收回金額是指資產的公平值減去處
  store assets which have impairment indicators. The recoverable           置成本與其使用價值兩者中的較高者。管理層
  amount is the higher of an asset’s fair value less cost of disposal      認為，相關直營店資產的可收回金額是通過使
  and its value-in-use. Management considers that the recoverable          用價值計算確定的，使用價值計算是基於管理
  amount of the assets of the relevant directly-managed stores is          層編製的銷售預測的貼現現金流預測值，主要
  determined by value-in-use calculations using discounted cash            假設包括預算銷售額和毛利額、收益增長率和
  flow projections based on sales forecast prepared by management          貼現率。
  with major assumptions including budgeted sales and gross profit,
  revenue growth rates and discount rates.

  Management estimated a recoverable amount of the directly-               管理層使用稅前貼現率17%估算直營店資產的
  managed store assets using a pre-tax discount rate of 17% which          可收回金額，該貼現率反映了當前市場對時間
  reflects current market assessment of time value and the specific        價值的評估以及與直營店有關的特定風險。
  risks relating to the directly-managed stores.

26 CONVERTIBLE NOTES                                                      26 可換股債券
  On 14 October 2020, pursuant to a subscription agreement dated             於二零二零年十月十四日，根據日期為二零二零
  14 September 2020 (the “Subscription Agreement”), the Company              年九月十四日之認購協議（「認購協議」），本公
  issued unlisted and unsecured RMB denominated, HK$ settled                 司發行以人民幣計值並以港元結算之二零二三
  convertible notes due in October 2023 in an aggregate principal            年十月到期非上市無抵押可換股債券，本金總
  amount of RMB25,300,000.                                                   額為人民幣25,300,000元。

  The terms of the convertible notes are summarised below:                   可換股債券條款概述如下：

  (a)   the convertible notes bear interest of 9.0% per annum on the         (a)   可換股債券按未贖回本金額按年利率9.0厘
        outstanding principal amount and the interest will be payable              計息，利息將於到期日贖回時支付，或可
        upon redemption on maturity date or convertible into shares                於轉換可換股債券時轉換為股份；
        upon conversion of the convertible notes;

  (b) the convertible notes are convertible at the option of the             (b) 於 或 自 可 換 股 債 券 發 行 日 期 起 至 到 期 日
      holder into fully paid ordinary shares on or after the issue date          止，可換股債券可由持有人選擇按換股價
      of the convertible notes up to maturity date at a conversion               每股0.221港元轉換為繳足股款普通股，固
      price of HK$0.221 per share, with fixed exchange rate at                   定匯率為1港元兌人民幣0.8836元，惟須根

        equal to the HK$ equivalent of the aggregate of 100% of the                回人民幣本金額加全部應計款項總和之港

        thereon.
